{"assignees":[],"body":"## Overview\n\nConvert the single-module `java/pom.xml` into a multi-module Maven reactor. Move the existing SDK code into a `sdk/` subdirectory while preserving its GAV (`com.github:copilot-sdk-java`).\n\n**This is task 4.1 of 9 in the implementation plan.** Tasks are assigned, completed, and merged serially in this listed order.\n\n**Branch:** `edburns/1917-java-embed-rust-cli-runtime-dd-3039924-agentic-run-02` on `upstream`\n\n## Plan and supporting resources\n\nOn the `edburns/1917-java-embed-rust-cli-runtime-dd-3039924-agentic-run-02` branch, the directory `1917-java-embed-rust-cli-runtime-remove-before-merge` contains the plan (`1917-embed-cli-runtime-ignorance-reduction-plan.md`) and supporting resources (spikes, prototypes, diagrams).\n\n**Read the entire plan before working.**\n\n## Relevant plan sections to carefully re-read\n\n- **Section 3.1 — Maven module structure for per-platform classifier JARs** — Resolution: Option B — hybrid multi-module reactor. The reactor structure is:\n  ```\n  java/\n  ├── pom.xml                          (parent, packaging=pom, new GAV: com.github:copilot-sdk-java-parent)\n  ├── sdk/\n  │   └── pom.xml                      (existing SDK, KEEPS GAV: com.github:copilot-sdk-java)\n  ├── copilot-native/\n  │   └── pom.xml                      (new GAV: com.github:copilot-sdk-java-runtime)\n  ├── copilot-native-all/\n  │   └── pom.xml                      (optional monolithic: com.github:copilot-sdk-java-runtime-all)\n  ```\n  Key decisions: the existing `copilot-sdk-java` GAV is preserved (no breaking change); the parent POM is `packaging=pom` and internal-only; no dependency from `copilot-sdk-java` to `copilot-sdk-java-runtime`.\n- **Section 4.1 — Parent POM restructure** (the primary task description)\n- **TDD discipline for all implementation steps** — every step must follow test-driven workflow: write tests first, implement until green, refactor, gate before proceeding.\n\n## Deliverables\n\n### Files to create\n\n1. **`java/pom.xml`** — New parent POM (`com.github:copilot-sdk-java-parent`, `packaging=pom`). Declares `<modules>` for `sdk`, `copilot-native`, and `copilot-native-all`. Centralizes shared properties, plugin versions, and `copilot.sdk.root` path. The `copilot-native` and `copilot-native-all` modules do NOT need to exist yet — they are created in later tasks. Include them in `<modules>` commented out or in a profile, so the reactor builds with just `sdk` for now.\n\n### Files to move\n\n2. **Existing `java/pom.xml` → `java/sdk/pom.xml`** — Add `<parent>` pointing to `copilot-sdk-java-parent`. Preserve existing GAV `com.github:copilot-sdk-java`. All existing source, test, and resource paths must resolve correctly from the new `java/sdk/` location.\n3. **Existing `java/src/` → `java/sdk/src/`**\n4. **Existing `java/config/` → `java/sdk/config/`** (or keep at `java/config/` and reference via `${project.parent.basedir}/config/` — choose whichever keeps paths simpler)\n\n### Files to update\n\n5. **`justfile`** — Update `java/` paths to `java/sdk/` where needed.\n6. **`.github/workflows/java-sdk-tests.yml`** — Update working directory references from `java/` to `java/sdk/` or `java/` as appropriate for the reactor.\n7. **Any other workflows referencing `java/pom.xml`** — search `.github/workflows/` for references to `java/pom.xml` or `java/` build commands and update them.\n\n## Gating tests and criteria\n\nAll of the following must pass before this task is considered complete:\n\n1. **Reactor build:** `mvn clean verify` from `java/` runs the full reactor successfully.\n2. **SDK-only build:** `mvn -pl sdk clean verify` from `java/` builds and tests the SDK exactly as before the restructure.\n3. **All existing tests pass:** Every existing unit test and integration test passes without modification (unless path changes require adjustment).\n4. **CI workflows work:** The updated workflow YAML files reference the correct directories and would run correctly.\n5. **GAV preservation:** The SDK artifact's GAV remains `com.github:copilot-sdk-java` — no consumer-visible change.\n6. **Spotless compliance:** `mvn spotless:check` passes.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Creating the `copilot-native` or `copilot-native-all` module directories or POMs (tasks 4.7 and later).\n- Any native binary handling, JNA dependencies, or FFI code.\n- Changes to Java source code (only build/project structure changes).\n","number":2167,"state":"OPEN","title":"[Java] Embed Rust CLI runtime 4.1: Parent POM restructure to multi-module reactor","url":"https://github.com/github/copilot-sdk/issues/2167"}

<sub>24s</sub>

### `bash`

**Wait for linked Copilot PR**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; TASK_ISSUE='2167'; BASE_BRANCH='edburns/1917-java-embed-rust-cli-runtime-dd-3039924-agentic-run-02'; TIMEOUT=900; INTERVAL=30; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  PR_NUMBER=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/issues/$TASK_ISSUE/timeline" --jq '.[] | select(.event == "cross-referenced") | select(.source.issue.pull_request != null) | select(.source.issue.state == "open") | .source.issue.number' 2>/dev/null | head -1)
  if [ -z "$PR_NUMBER" ]; then PR_NUMBER=$(gh pr list -R "$REPO" --state open --limit 100 --json number,body --jq ".[] | select((.body // \"\") | test(\"#$TASK_ISSUE\")) | .number" | head -1); fi
  if [ -z "$PR_NUMBER" ]; then PR_NUMBER=$(gh pr list -R "$REPO" --state open --limit 100 --json number,title,headRefName --jq ".[] | select((.title | test(\"$TASK_ISSUE\"; \"i\")) or (.headRefName | test(\"$TASK_ISSUE\"))) | .number" | head -1); fi
  if [ -n "$PR_NUMBER" ]; then
    gh pr view "$PR_NUMBER" -R "$REPO" --json number,title,state,isDraft,baseRefName,headRefName,headRefOid,url
    ACTUAL_BASE=$(gh pr view "$PR_NUMBER" -R "$REPO" --json baseRefName --jq '.baseRefName')
    [ "$ACTUAL_BASE" = "$BASE_BRANCH" ] || { echo "ERROR: wrong base $ACTUAL_BASE"; exit 1; }
    OWNER=${REPO%%/*}; NAME=${REPO#*/}
    LINKED=$(gh api graphql -f query='query($owner:String!,$name:String!,$number:Int!){repository(owner:$owner,name:$name){pullRequest(number:$number){closingIssuesReferences(first:100){nodes{number}}}}}' -F owner="$OWNER" -F name="$NAME" -F number="$PR_NUMBER" --jq ".data.repository.pullRequest.closingIssuesReferences.nodes[] | select(.number == $TASK_ISSUE) | .number")
    [ "$LINKED" = "$TASK_ISSUE" ] || { echo 'ERROR: PR lacks authoritative issue link'; exit 2; }
    echo "PR_NUMBER=$PR_NUMBER"
    exit 0
  fi
  sleep "$INTERVAL"; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED + INTERVAL))
done
echo 'ERROR: no PR created within 15 minutes'; exit 3

while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  DATA=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json state,isDraft,body,headRefOid)
  echo "$DATA" | jq '{state,isDraft,headRefOid,body}'
  LINKED=$(gh api graphql -f query='query($owner:String!,$name:String!,$number:Int!){repository(owner:$owner,name:$name){pullRequest(number:$number){closingIssuesReferences(first:100){nodes{number}}}}}' -F owner="$OWNER" -F name="$NAME" -F number="$PR" --jq ".data.repository.pullRequest.closingIssuesReferences.nodes[] | select(.number == $TASK) | .number")
  if [ "$LINKED" = "$TASK" ]; then echo "LINKED_TASK=$LINKED"; exit 0; fi
  sleep "$INTERVAL"; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED + INTERVAL))
done
echo "ERROR: PR #$PR does not close task issue #$TASK after ${TIMEOUT}s"; exit 2

All existing source, test, and resource paths must resolve correctly from the new `java/sdk/` location.\n> 3. **Existing `java/src/` → `java/sdk/src/`**\n> 4. **Existing `java/config/` → `java/sdk/config/`** (or keep at `java/config/` and reference via `${project.parent.basedir}/config/` — choose whichever keeps paths simpler)\n> \n> ### Files to update\n> \n> 5. **`justfile`** — Update `java/` paths to `java/sdk/` where needed.\n> 6. **`.github/workflows/java-sdk-tests.yml`** — Update working directory references from `java/` to `java/sdk/` or `java/` as appropriate for the reactor.\n> 7. **Any other workflows referencing `java/pom.xml`** — search `.github/workflows/` for references to `java/pom.xml` or `java/` build commands and update them.\n> \n> ## Gating tests and criteria\n> \n> All of the following must pass before this task is considered complete:\n> \n> 1. **Reactor build:** `mvn clean verify` from `java/` runs the full reactor successfully.\n> 2. **SDK-only build:** `mvn -pl sdk clean verify` from `java/` builds and tests the SDK exactly as before the restructure.\n> 3. **All existing tests pass:** Every existing unit test and integration test passes without modification (unless path changes require adjustment).\n> 4. **CI workflows work:** The updated workflow YAML files reference the correct directories and would run correctly.\n> 5. **GAV preservation:** The SDK artifact's GAV remains `com.github:copilot-sdk-java` — no consumer-visible change.\n> 6. **Spotless compliance:** `mvn spotless:check` passes.\n> \n> ## Out of scope\n> \n> - Creating the `copilot-native` or `copilot-native-all` module directories or POMs (tasks 4.7 and later).\n> - Any native binary handling, JNA dependencies, or FFI code.\n> - Changes to Java source code (only build/project structure changes).\n> </issue_description>\n> - Closes github/copilot-sdk#2167\n> \n> ## Comments on the Issue (you are @copilot in this section)\n> \n> <comments>\n> </comments>\n- Fixes #2167"

<sub>11m 40s</sub>

### `bash`

**Wait for Copilot implementation**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; PR=2170; TIMEOUT=7200; INTERVAL=30; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  PR_STATE=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json state --jq '.state')
  IS_DRAFT=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json isDraft --jq '.isDraft')
  [ "$PR_STATE" = 'OPEN' ] && [ "$IS_DRAFT" = 'true' ] || { echo 'ERROR: PR no longer open and draft'; exit 4; }
  TIMELINE=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/issues/$PR/timeline?per_page=100" -H 'Accept: application/vnd.github+json')
  LATEST_START=$(printf '%s' "$TIMELINE" | jq -r '[.[] | select(.event == "copilot_work_started") | .created_at] | max // empty')
  LATEST_FINISH=$(printf '%s' "$TIMELINE" | jq -r '[.[] | select(.event == "copilot_work_finished") | .created_at] | max // empty')
  echo "elapsed=$ELAPSED start=${LATEST_START:-none} finish=${LATEST_FINISH:-none}"
  if [ -n "$LATEST_START" ] && [ -n "$LATEST_FINISH" ] && { [[ "$LATEST_FINISH" > "$LATEST_START" ]] || [ "$LATEST_FINISH" = "$LATEST_START" ]; }; then
    PR_JSON=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR")
    CHANGED_FILES=$(printf '%s' "$PR_JSON" | jq -r '.changed_files'); BASE_SHA=$(printf '%s' "$PR_JSON" | jq -r '.base.sha'); HEAD_SHA=$(printf '%s' "$PR_JSON" | jq -r '.head.sha')
    PR_FILE_COUNT=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/files?per_page=100" --paginate --jq '.[].filename' | wc -l)
    BASE_TREE=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/git/commits/$BASE_SHA" --jq '.tree.sha'); HEAD_TREE=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/git/commits/$HEAD_SHA" --jq '.tree.sha')
    [ "$CHANGED_FILES" -gt 0 ] && [ "$PR_FILE_COUNT" -gt 0 ] && [ "$BASE_TREE" != "$HEAD_TREE" ] || { echo 'ERROR: no effective changes'; exit 6; }
    echo "CCA_COMPLETE HEAD_SHA=$HEAD_SHA CHANGED_FILES=$CHANGED_FILES PR_FILE_COUNT=$PR_FILE_COUNT"
    exit 0
  fi
  sleep "$INTERVAL"; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED + INTERVAL))
done
echo 'ERROR: CCA completion timeout'; exit 5

<sub>49m 49s</sub>

### `bash`

**Approve and watch PR workflows**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; BRANCH='copilot/edburns1917-java-embed-rust-cli-runtime-dd-3039924'; PR=2170
PENDING_RUNS=$(gh run list -R "$REPO" --branch "$BRANCH" --limit 100 --json databaseId,conclusion --jq '.[] | select(.conclusion == "action_required") | .databaseId')
for RUN_ID in $PENDING_RUNS; do
  success=false
  for attempt in 1 2 3; do
    if gh run rerun "$RUN_ID" -R "$REPO"; then success=true; break; fi
    sleep 10
  done
  [ "$success" = true ] || { echo "ERROR: failed to rerun $RUN_ID"; exit 1; }
done
echo "Approved runs: ${PENDING_RUNS:-none}"
gh pr checks "$PR" -R "$REPO" --watch --fail-fast

<sub>53m 7s</sub>

### `bash`

**Request workflow lock fixes**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; PR=2170; CURRENT_SHA='2ae1ad643995c49f03deb2100a1851e8312a3cd7'
BODY=$(cat <<'EOF'
@copilot Please fix the following issue:

## CI Failure: Verify compiled workflows

The `Check for uncommitted changes` step reports these generated lock files are stale after their workflow source files were updated:

- `.github/workflows/java-adapt-handwritten-code-to-accept-upgrade-changes.lock.yml`
- `.github/workflows/java-codegen-fix.lock.yml`
- `.github/workflows/sdk-consistency-review.lock.yml`

The run says: `Lock files are out of date. Run 'gh aw compile' and commit the results.`

**Fix:** Regenerate the affected agentic workflow lock files with `gh aw compile`, commit them, and ensure the compiled-workflows check is clean. Do not hand-edit the generated lock files.
EOF
)
gh pr review "$PR" -R "$REPO" --request-changes --body "$BODY"
TIMEOUT=600; INTERVAL=30; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  NEW_SHA=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json headRefOid --jq '.headRefOid')
  if [ "$NEW_SHA" != "$CURRENT_SHA" ]; then echo "NEW_SHA=$NEW_SHA"; exit 0; fi
  sleep "$INTERVAL"; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED + INTERVAL))
done
echo 'ERROR: Copilot did not push within 10 minutes'; exit 8

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; PR=2170; TIMEOUT=7200; INTERVAL=30; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  META=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json state,isDraft,headRefOid)
  [ "$(printf '%s' "$META" | jq -r .state)" = OPEN ] && [ "$(printf '%s' "$META" | jq -r .isDraft)" = true ] || { echo 'ERROR: PR no longer open/draft'; exit 4; }
  SHA=$(printf '%s' "$META" | jq -r .headRefOid)
  TIMELINE=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/issues/$PR/timeline?per_page=100" -H 'Accept: application/vnd.github+json')
  START=$(printf '%s' "$TIMELINE" | jq -r '[.[] | select(.event=="copilot_work_started") | .created_at] | max // empty')
  FINISH=$(printf '%s' "$TIMELINE" | jq -r '[.[] | select(.event=="copilot_work_finished") | .created_at] | max // empty')
  echo "elapsed=$ELAPSED sha=$SHA start=${START:-none} finish=${FINISH:-none}"
  if [ "$SHA" != '2ae1ad643995c49f03deb2100a1851e8312a3cd7' ] && [ -n "$START" ] && [ -n "$FINISH" ] && { [[ "$FINISH" > "$START" ]] || [ "$FINISH" = "$START" ]; }; then
    gh api "/repos/$REPO/pulls/$PR/commits?per_page=100" --paginate --jq '.[-1] | {sha:.sha,message:.commit.message}'
    echo "VALIDATION_SHA=$SHA"; exit 0
  fi
  sleep "$INTERVAL"; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED+INTERVAL))
done
exit 5

@copilot The compiled-workflows check still fails because the lock files were generated with the wrong gh-aw compiler version.

## CI Failure: Verify compiled workflows

Current committed lock metadata says `compiler_version: v0.82.10`, but CI compiles with `v0.83.1` and produces a 12-file diff (568 insertions, 527 deletions), including all three relevant lock files.

**Fix:** Install/use gh-aw compiler **v0.83.1**, run `gh aw compile` with that exact version, and commit the resulting generated files. Confirm the committed lock metadata reports `compiler_version: v0.83.1` and that compiling again with v0.83.1 leaves no diff. Do not use the currently installed v0.82.10 compiler.
EOF
)

  if [ "$SHA" != "$CURRENT" ]; then echo "NEW_SHA=$SHA"; exit 0; fi
  sleep 30; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED+30))
done
echo 'ERROR: no Copilot update within 10 minutes'; exit 8

**Wait for corrected lock cycle**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; PR=2170; OLD='5c7c2523582e801be9ed902e35db69788f36ef61'; TIMEOUT=7200; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do
  META=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json state,isDraft,headRefOid)
  [ "$(echo "$META"|jq -r .state)" = OPEN ] && [ "$(echo "$META"|jq -r .isDraft)" = true ] || exit 4
  SHA=$(echo "$META"|jq -r .headRefOid)
  TL=$(gh api "/repos/$REPO/issues/$PR/timeline?per_page=100" -H 'Accept: application/vnd.github+json')
  S=$(echo "$TL"|jq -r '[.[]|select(.event=="copilot_work_started")|.created_at]|max//empty'); F=$(echo "$TL"|jq -r '[.[]|select(.event=="copilot_work_finished")|.created_at]|max//empty')
  echo "elapsed=$ELAPSED sha=$SHA start=$S finish=$F"
  if [ "$SHA" != "$OLD" ] && [ -n "$S" ] && [ -n "$F" ] && { [[ "$F" > "$S" ]] || [ "$F" = "$S" ]; }; then echo "VALIDATION_SHA=$SHA"; exit 0; fi
  sleep 30; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED+30))
done
exit 5

<sub>81m 54s</sub>

### `bash`

**Request CI-equivalent workflow compilation**

$ set -o pipefail
REPO='github/copilot-sdk'; PR=2170; CURRENT='01df301dca05af20741e80d47075198ffa9a1cce'
BODY=$(cat <<'EOF'
@copilot The compiled-workflows check still fails because CI compiles with action pinning, while the committed output leaves `github/gh-aw-actions/setup@v0.83.1` unpinned.

## CI Failure: Verify compiled workflows

CI changes 13 files (105 insertions, 95 deletions). For example it changes:

`uses: github/gh-aw-actions/setup@v0.83.1`

to:

`uses: github/gh-aw-actions/setup@8bdba8075360648fe6802302a5b4e016361dc6ac # v0.83.1`

and updates the `gh-aw-manifest` SHA accordingly.

**Fix:** Reproduce the exact command/options used by `.github/workflows/verify-compiled.yml` (including action pinning), using gh-aw v0.83.1, commit the complete generated diff, then rerun that exact compile command and verify `git diff` is empty.
EOF
)
gh pr review "$PR" -R "$REPO" --request-changes --body "$BODY"
TIMEOUT=600; ELAPSED=0
while [ "$ELAPSED" -lt "$TIMEOUT" ]; do SHA=$(gh pr view "$PR" -R "$REPO" --json headRefOid --jq .headRefOid); if [ "$SHA" != "$CURRENT" ]; then echo "NEW_SHA=$SHA"; exit 0; fi; sleep 30; ELAPSED=$((ELAPSED+30)); done
exit 8
